Architecture Decision\n    40→\n    41→**Chosen Approach: Centralized Layout Engine with Strategy Pattern**\n    42→\n    43→**Rationale:**\n    44→1. **Single Source of Truth** - One `WindowLayoutEngine` module handles all positioning logic\n    45→2. **Strategy Pattern** - Pluggable layout algorithms (Pillars, Quads, future: Cascade, Fullscreen)\n    46→3. **Live Reactivity** - Polling-based window detection triggers automatic re-layout\n    47→4. **Multi-Monitor First** - Treat single screen as special case of multi-monitor\n    48→5. **Configuration-Driven** - Layout mode stored in state, user-switchable via hotkey\n    49→\n    50→**Trade-offs:**\n    51→- **Chosen:** Complexity upfront for long-term maintainability\n    52→- **Rejected:** Quick fixes to existing code (would accumulate tech debt)\n    53→- **Chosen:** Polling for window changes (simple, reliable)\n    54→- **Rejected:** Event-driven (complex Windows API hooks)\n    55→\n    56→## 3. handles (skip and continue)\n   335→- [ ] Add verbose logging (integrates with US-009)\n   336→\n   337→## 7. Critical Details\n   338→\n   339→### 7.1 Pillars Layout Algorithm\n   340→\n   341→```powershell\n   342→function Get-PillarsLayout {\n   343→    param(\n   344→        [int]$WindowCount,\n   345→        [array]$Screens,\n   346→        [int]$MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4,\n   347→        [int]$GapSize = 60\n   348→    )\n   349→\n   350→    # Step 1: Distribute windows across screens\n   351→    $distribution = Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount $WindowCount `\n   352→                                          -ScreenCount $Screens.Count `\n   353→                                          -MaxPerScreen $MaxPillarsPerScreen\n   354→\n   355→    $rectangles = @()\n   356→    $windowIndex = 0\n   357→\n   358→    # Step 2: For each screen, layout its assigned windows\n   359→    for ($screenIdx = 0; $screenIdx -lt $Screens.Count; $screenIdx++) {\n   360→        $screen = $Screens[$screenIdx]\n   361→        $windowsOnScreen = $distribution[$screenIdx]\n   362→\n   363→        if ($windowsOnScreen -eq 0) { continue }\n   364→\n   365→        # Step 3: Determine if single row or multi-row\n   366→        if ($windowsOnScreen -le $MaxPillarsPerScreen) {\n   367→            $columns = $windowsOnScreen\n   368→            $rows = 1\n   369→        } else {\n   370→            $columns = $MaxPillarsPerScreen\n   371→            $rows = [Math]::Ceiling($windowsOnScreen / $columns)\n   372→        }\n   373→\n   374→        # Step 4: Calculate cell dimensions\n   375→        $totalHGaps = ($columns + 1) * $GapSize\n   376→        $totalVGaps = ($rows + 1) * $GapSize\n   377→        $cellWidth = [int](($screen.Width - $totalHGaps) / $columns)\n   378→        $cellHeight = [int](($screen.Height - $totalVGaps) / $rows)\n   379→\n   380→        # Step 5: Place each window in grid\n   381→        for ($i = 0; ] Handle invalid window handles (skip and continue)\n- [ ] Add verbose logging (integrates with US-009)\n\n## 7. Critical Details\n\n### 7.1 Pillars Layout Algorithm\n\n```powershell\nfunction Get-PillarsLayout {\n    param(\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n        [array]$Screens,\n        [int]$MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4,\n        [int]$GapSize = 60\n    )\n\n    # Step 1: Distribute windows across screens\n    $distribution = Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount $WindowCount `\n                                          -ScreenCount $Screens.Count `\n                                          -MaxPerScreen $MaxPillarsPerScreen\n\n    $rectangles = @()\n    $windowIndex = 0\n\n    # Step 2: For each screen, layout its assigned windows\n    for ($screenIdx = 0; $screenIdx -lt $Screens.Count; $screenIdx++) {\n        $screen = $Screens[$screenIdx]\n        $windowsOnScreen = $distribution[$screenIdx]\n\n        if ($windowsOnScreen -eq 0) { continue }\n\n        # Step 3: Determine if single row or multi-row\n        if ($windowsOnScreen -le $MaxPillarsPerScreen) {\n            $columns = $windowsOnScreen\n            $rows = 1\n        } else {\n            $columns = $MaxPillarsPerScreen\n            $rows = [Math]::Ceiling($windowsOnScreen / $columns)\n        }\n\n        # Step 4: Calculate cell dimensions\n        $totalHGaps = ($columns + 1) * $GapSize\n        $totalVGaps = ($rows + 1) * $GapSize\n        $cellWidth = [int](($screen.Width - $totalHGaps) / $columns)\n        $cellHeight = [int](($screen.Height - $totalVGaps) / $rows)\n\n        # Step 5: Place each window in grid\n        for ($i = 0; $i -lt $windowsOnScreen; $i++) {\n            $col = $i % $columns\n            $row = [Math]::Floor($i / $columns)\n\n            $x = $screen.Left + $GapSize + ($col * ($cellWidth + $GapSize))\n            $y = $screen.Top + $GapSize + ($row * ($cellHeight + $GapSize))\n\n            $rectangles += @{\n                X = $x\n                Y = $y\n                Width = $cellWidth\n                Height = $cellHeight\n                ScreenIndex = $screenIdx\n                WindowIndex = $windowIndex++\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return $rectangles\n}\n```\n\n### 7.2 Quads Layout Algorithm\n\n```powershell\nfunction Get-QuadsLayout {\n    param(\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n        [array]$Screens,\n        [int]$GapSize = 60\n    )\n\n    $rectangles = @()\n    $windowIndex = 0\n    $windowsPerQuad = 4\n\n    # Calculate how many quads needed\n    $quadsNeeded = [Math]::Ceiling($WindowCount / $windowsPerQuad)\n    $quadsPerScreen = [Math]::Ceiling($quadsNeeded / $Screens.Count)\n\n    for ($screenIdx = 0; $screenIdx -lt $Screens.Count; $screenIdx++) {\n        $screen = $Screens[$screenIdx]\n\n        # Plus-gap: horizontal gap in middle, vertical gap in middle\n        $halfWidth = [int](($screen.Width - (3 * $GapSize)) / 2)\n        $halfHeight = [int](($screen.Height - (3 * $GapSize)) / 2)\n\n        # Positions: TL, TR, BL, BR\n        $quadPositions = @(\n            @{ X = $screen.Left + $GapSize; Y = $screen.Top + $GapSize },\n            @{ X = $screen.Left + (2*$GapSize) + $halfWidth; Y = $screen.Top + $GapSize },\n            @{ X = $screen.Left + $GapSize; Y = $screen.Top + (2*$GapSize) + $halfHeight },\n            @{ X = $screen.Left + (2*$GapSize) + $halfWidth; Y = $screen.Top + (2*$GapSize) + $halfHeight }\n        )\n\n        for ($posIdx = 0; $posIdx -lt 4; $posIdx++) {\n            if ($windowIndex -ge $WindowCount) { break }\n\n            $rectangles += @{\n                X = $quadPositions[$posIdx].X\n                Y = $quadPositions[$posIdx].Y\n                Width = $halfWidth\n                Height = $halfHeight\n                ScreenIndex = $screenIdx\n                WindowIndex = $windowIndex++\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return $rectangles\n}\n```\n\n### 7.3 Window Distribution Algorithm\n\n```powershell\nfunction Get-WindowDistribution {\n    param(\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n        [int]$ScreenCount,\n        [int]$MaxPerScreen = 4\n    )\n\n    $distribution = @(0) * $ScreenCount\n    $windowsPerScreen = [Math]::Floor($WindowCount / $ScreenCount)\n    $remainder = $WindowCount % $ScreenCount\n\n    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $ScreenCount; $i++) {\n        $distribution[$i] = [Math]::Min($windowsPerScreen, $MaxPerScreen)\n    }\n\n    # Distribute remainder\n    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $remainder; $i++) {\n        if ($distribution[$i] -lt $MaxPerScreen) {\n            $distribution[$i]++\n        }\n    }\n\n    return $distribution\n}\n```\n\n## 8. 64→\"@\n    65→\n    66→# Load System.Windows.Forms for screen detection\n    67→Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n    68→\n    69→# --- SCREEN TOPOLOGY N windows across M screens with a maximum per screen.\n   132→\n   133→.DESCRIPTION\n   134→    Evenly distributes windows across available screens, respecting MaxPerScreen limit.\n   135→    Algorithm: floor division with remainder distributed to first screens.\n   136→\n   137→.PARAMETER WindowCount\n   138→    Total number of windows to distribute\n   139→\n   140→.PARAMETER ScreenCount\n   141→    Number of available screens\n   142→\n   143→.PARAMETER MaxPerScreen\n   144→    Maximum windows allowed per screen (default: 4)\n   145→\n   146→.OUTPUTS\n   147→    Array of 153→.EXAMPLE\n   154→    Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount 5 -ScreenCount 1 -MaxPerScreen 4\n   155→    # Returns: @(4) - overflow not handled in distribution, handled by layout strategy\n   156→\n   157→.EXAMPLE\n   158→    Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount 8 -ScreenCount 2 -MaxPerScreen 4\n   159→    # Returns: @(4, 4)\n   160→\n   161→.EXAMPLE\n   162→    Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount 182→        return @()\n   183→    }\n   184→\n   185→    # Initialize distribution array\n   186→    $distribution = @(0) * $ScreenCount\n   187→\n   188→    # Special case: single screen - 193→\n   194→    # Multi-screen distribution: balance windows across screens\n   195→    # Base windows per screen (floor division)\n   196→    $windowsPerScreen = [Math]::Floor($WindowCount / $screenIndex = $i % $ScreenCount\n   212→        if ($distribution[$screenIndex] -lt $MaxPerScreen) {\n   213→            $distribution[$screenIndex]++\n   214→        }\n   215→        else {\n $distribution\n   227→}\n   228→\n   229→# --- PILLARS LAYOUT STRATEGY ---\n   230→\n   231→<#\n   232→.SYNOPSIS\n   233→    Calculate Pillars layout: vertical columns arranged side-by-side.\n   234→\n   235→.DESCRIPTION\n   236→    Arranges windows as vertical pillars (columns) with configurable gaps.\n   237→    Supports multi-row overflow and multi-monitor distribution.\n   238→\n   239→    Layout logic:\n   240→    - If windowsOnScreen <= MaxPillarsPerScreen: single row of N columns\n   241→    - If windowsOnScreen > MaxPillarsPerScreen: grid with MaxPillarsPerScreen columns, multiple rows\n   242→    - Gap spacing: (N+1) gaps for N columns, (M+1) gaps for M rows\n   243→\n   244→.PARAMETER WindowCount\n   245→    Total number of windows to layout\n   246→\n   247→.PARAMETER Screens\n   248→    Array of screen objects from Get-ScreenTopology\n   249→\n   250→.PARAMETER MaxPillarsPerScreen\n   251→    Maximum pillars (columns) per screen in a single row (default: 4)\n   252→\n   253→.PARAMETER GapSize\n   254→    Pixel gap between windows and screen edges (default: 60)\n   255→\n   256→.OUTPUTS\n   257→    Array of @{ X, Y, Width, Height, ScreenIndex, WindowIndex }\n   258→\n   259→.EXAMPLE\n   260→    Get-PillarsLayout -WindowCount 4 -Screens $screens -MaxPillarsPerScreen 4 -GapSize 60\n   261→    # Returns 4 rectangles in a single row, evenly distributed with 60px gaps\n   262→#>\n   263→function Get-PillarsLayout {\n   264→    param(\n   265→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n   266→        [int]$WindowCount,\n   267→\n   268→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n   269→        [array]$Screens,\n   270→\n   271→        [int]$MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4,\n   272→\n   273→        [int]$GapSize = 60\n   274→    )\n   275→\n   276→    if ($WindowCount -le 0) {\n   277→        return @()\n   278→    }\n   279→\n   280→    if ($Screens.Count -eq 0) {\n   281→        Write-Warning \"No screens available for layout\"\n   282→        return @()\n   283→    }\n   284→\n   285→    # Step 1: Distribute windows across screens\n   286→    $distribution = Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount $WindowCount `\n   287→                                          -ScreenCount $Screens.Count `\n   288→                                          -MaxPerScreen $MaxPillarsPerScreen\n   289→\n   290→    $rectangles = @()\n   291→    $windowIndex = 0\n   292→\n   293→    # Step 2: For each screen, layout its assigned windows\n   294→    for ($screenIdx = 0; $screenIdx -lt $Screens.Count; $screenIdx++) {\n   295→        $screen = $Screens[$screenIdx]\n   296→        $windowsOnScreen = $distribution[$screenIdx]\n   297→\n   298→        if ($windowsOnScreen -eq 0) {\n   299→            continue\n   300→        }\n   301→\n   302→        # Step 3: Determine if single row or multi-row\n   303→        if ($windowsOnScreen -le $MaxPillarsPerScreen) {\n   304→            # Single row layout\n   305→            $columns = $windowsOnScreen\n   306→            $rows = 1\n   307→        } else {\n   308→            # Multi-row layout: 4 columns, multiple rows\n   309→            $columns = $MaxPillarsPerScreen\n   310→            $rows = [Math]::Ceiling($windowsOnScreen / $columns)\n   311→        }\n   312→\n   313→        # Step 4: Calculate cell dimensions\n   314→        # Formula: (N+1) gaps for N columns/rows\n   315→        $totalHGaps = ($columns + 1) * $GapSize\n   316→        $totalVGaps = ($rows + 1) * $GapSize\n   317→        $cellWidth = [int](($screen.Width - $totalHGaps) / $columns)\n   318→        $cellHeight = [int](($screen.Height - $totalVGaps) / $rows)\n   319→\n   320→        # Step 5: Place each window in grid\n   321→        for ($i = 0; $i -lt $windowsOnScreen; $i++) {\n   322→            # Calculate grid position (column-major order)\n   323→            $col = $i % $columns\n   324→            $row = [Math]::Floor($i / $columns)\n   325→\n   326→            # Calculate pixel position\n   327→            # Formula: edge gap + (cell_index * (cell_size + gap))\n   328→            $x = $screen.Left + $GapSize + ($col * ($cellWidth + $GapSize))\n   329→            $y = $screen.Top + $GapSize + ($row * ($cellHeight + $GapSize))\n   330→\n   331→            $rectangles += @{\n   332→                X = $x\n   333→                Y = $y\n   334→                Width = $cellWidth\n   335→                Height = $cellHeight\n   336→                ScreenIndex = $screenIdx\n   337→                WindowIndex = $windowIndex++\n   338→            }\n   339→        }\n   340→    }\n   341→\n   342→    return $rectangles\n   343→}\n   344→\n   345→# --- QUADS LAYOUT STRATEGY ---\n   346→\n   347→<#\n   348→.SYNOPSIS\n   349→    Calculate Quads layout: 2x2 grid with plus-shaped gap in center.\n   350→\n   351→.DESCRIPTION\n   352→    Arranges windows in a 2x2 grid pattern with a distinctive plus-shaped (+) gap\n   353→    in the center where horizontal and vertical gaps intersect.\n   354→\n   355→    Layout pattern (single screen, 4 windows):\n   356→    ┌─────┐ GAP ┌─────┐\n   357→    │  1  │     │  2  │\n   358→    └─────┘     └─────┘\n   359→       GAP   +   GAP\n   360→    ┌─────┐     ┌─────┘\n   361→    │  3  │     │  4  │\n   362→    └─────┘     └─────┘\n   363→\n   364→    Window placement order: Top-Left, Top-Right, Bottom-Left, Bottom-Right\n   365→\n   366→    For fewer than 4 windows:\n   367→    - 1 window: Top-Left only\n   368→    - 2 windows: Top row (TL, TR)\n   369→    - 3 windows: Top row + Bottom-Left\n   370→\n   371→    For more than 4 windows:\n   372→    - Overflow to additional screens (if available)\n   373→    - Each screen gets up to 4 windows in quad pattern\n   374→\n   375→.PARAMETER WindowCount\n   376→    Total number of windows to layout\n   377→\n   378→.PARAMETER Screens\n   379→    Array of screen objects from Get-ScreenTopology\n   380→\n   381→.PARAMETER GapSize\n   382→    Pixel gap between windows and screen edges (default: 60)\n   383→    This creates the plus-shaped gap in the center\n   384→\n   385→.OUTPUTS\n   386→    Array of @{ X, Y, Width, Height, ScreenIndex, WindowIndex }\n   387→\n   388→.EXAMPLE\n   389→    Get-QuadsLayout -WindowCount 4 -Screens $screens -GapSize 60\n   390→    # Returns 4 rectangles in 2x2 grid with 60px plus-gap\n   391→\n   392→.EXAMPLE\n   393→    Get-QuadsLayout -WindowCount 2 -Screens $screens -GapSize 60\n   394→    # Returns 2 rectangles in top row only\n   395→\n   396→.NOTES\n   397→    Plus-gap calculation:\n   398→    - Each quadrant: (screen_dimension - 3*gap) / 2\n   399→    - Three gaps: left edge, center, right edge (or top, center, bottom)\n   400→    - Center gap creates the distinctive + pattern\n   401→#>\n   402→function Get-QuadsLayout {\n   403→    param(\n   404→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n   405→        [int]$WindowCount,\n   406→\n   407→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n   408→        [array]$Screens,\n   409→\n   410→        [int]$GapSize = 60\n   411→    )\n   412→\n   413→    if ($WindowCount -le 0) {\n   414→        return @()\n   415→    }\n   416→\n   417→    if ($Screens.Count -eq 0) {\n   418→        Write-Warning \"No screens available for layout\"\n   419→        return @()\n   420→    }\n   421→\n   422→    $rectangles = @()\n   423→    $windowIndex = 0\n   424→    $windowsPerQuad = 4\n   425→\n   426→    # Calculate how many screens needed (4 windows per quad per screen)\n   427→    $windowsRemaining = $WindowCount\n   428→\n   429→    for ($screenIdx = 0; $screenIdx -lt $Screens.Count; $screenIdx++) {\n   430→        if ($windowsRemaining -le 0) {\n   431→            break\n   432→        }\n   433→\n   434→        $screen = $Screens[$screenIdx]\n   435→\n   436→        # Determine how many windows on this screen (max 4 per quad)\n   437→        $windowsOnThisScreen = [Math]::Min($windowsRemaining, $windowsPerQuad)\n   438→\n   439→        # Plus-gap calculation:\n   440→        # Each dimension has 3 gaps: edge + center + edge\n   441→        # Two quadrants share the remaining space equally\n   442→        # Formula: (total - 3*gap) / 2\n   443→        $halfWidth = [int](($screen.Width - (3 * $GapSize)) / 2)\n   444→        $halfHeight = [int](($screen.Height - (3 * $GapSize)) / 2)\n   445→\n   446→        # Define quad positions: TL, TR, BL, BR\n   447→        $quadPositions = @(\n   448→            @{ X = $screen.Left + $GapSize; Y = $screen.Top + $GapSize; Label = \"TL\" },\n   449→            @{ X = $screen.Left + (2 * $GapSize) + $halfWidth; Y = $screen.Top + $GapSize; Label = \"TR\" },\n   450→            @{ X = $screen.Left + $GapSize; Y = $screen.Top + (2 * $GapSize) + $halfHeight; Label = \"BL\" },\n   451→            @{ X = $screen.Left + (2 * $GapSize) + $halfWidth; Y = $screen.Top + (2 * $GapSize) + $halfHeight; Label = \"BR\" }\n   452→        )\n   453→\n   454→        # Place windows in order: TL, TR, BL, BR\n   455→        for ($posIdx = 0; $posIdx -lt $windowsOnThisScreen; $posIdx++) {\n   456→            $rectangles += @{\n   457→                X = $quadPositions[$posIdx].X\n   458→                Y = $quadPositions[$posIdx].Y\n   459→                Width = $halfWidth\n   460→                Height = $halfHeight\n   461→                ScreenIndex = $screenIdx\n   462→                WindowIndex = $windowIndex++\n   463→            }\n   464→        }\n   465→\n   466→        $windowsRemaining -= $windowsOnThisScreen\n   467→    }\n   468→\n   469→    # Force return as array (PowerShell unwraps single-element arrays)\n   470→    return @(,$rectangles)\n   471→}\n   472→\n   473→# --- CONFIGURATION MANAGEMENT ---\n   474→\n   475→<#\n   476→.SYNOPSIS\n   477→    Read layout configuration from matrix_state.json.\n   478→\n   479→.DESCRIPTION\n   480→    Loads layout preferences from persistent state file. Returns defaults\n   481→    if config is missing or invalid. Implements US-002 error handling pattern.\n   482→\n   483→.OUTPUTS\n   484→    Hashtable with layout configuration:\n   485→    @{ Mode = 'Pillars'; MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4; GapSize = 60; PreferredScreen = 0 }\n   486→\n   487→.EXAMPLE\n   488→    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n   489→    # Returns: @{ Mode = 'Pillars'; MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4; GapSize = 60; PreferredScreen = 0 }\n   490→\n   491→.NOTES\n   492→    Default configuration:\n   493→    - Mode: Pillars (vertical columns layout)\n   494→    - MaxPillarsPerScreen: 4 (max columns per screen)\n   495→    - GapSize: 60 (pixels between windows and edges)\n   496→    - PreferredScreen: 0 (primary screen)\n   497→#>\n   498→function Get-MatrixLayoutConfig {\n   499→    $stateFilePath = \"$env:USERPROFILE\\Documents\\Matrix\\matrix_state.json\"\n   500→\n   501→    # Default configuration\n   502→    $defaultConfig = @{\n   503→        Mode = 'Pillars'\n   504→        MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4\n   505→        GapSize = 60\n   506→        PreferredScreen = 0\n   507→    }\n   508→\n   509→    # Try to load existing state (US-002 pattern: JSON error handling)\n   510→    try {\n   511→        if (Test-Path $stateFilePath) {\n   512→            $stateJson = Get-Content -Path $stateFilePath -Raw -ErrorAction Stop\n   513→            $state = $stateJson | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop\n   514→\n   515→            # Extract layout config if present\n   516→            if ($state.layout) {\n   517→                $config = @{\n   518→                    Mode = if ($state.layout.mode) { $state.layout.mode } else { $defaultConfig.Mode }\n   519→                    MaxPillarsPerScreen = if ($state.layout.maxPillarsPerScreen) { $state.layout.maxPillarsPerScreen } else { $defaultConfig.MaxPillarsPerScreen }\n   520→                    GapSize = if ($state.layout.gapSize) { $state.layout.gapSize } else { $defaultConfig.GapSize }\n   521→                    PreferredScreen = if ($state.layout.preferredScreen -ne $null) { $state.layout.preferredScreen } else { $defaultConfig.PreferredScreen }\n   522→                }\n   523→                return $config\n   524→            }\n   525→        }\n   526→\n   527→        # No config found, return defaults\n   528→        return $defaultConfig\n   529→    }\n   530→    catch {\n   531→        Write-Warning \"Failed to load layout config from matrix_state.json: $_\"\n   532→        Write-Warning \"Using default configuration\"\n   533→        return $defaultConfig\n   534→    }\n   535→}\n   536→\n   537→<#\n   538→.SYNOPSIS\n   539→    Write layout configuration to matrix_state.json.\n   540→\n   541→.DESCRIPTION\n   542→    Persists layout preferences to state file. Uses atomic write pattern (US-001)\n   543→    to prevent corruption. Merges with existing state to preserve other data.\n   544→\n   545→.PARAMETER Config\n   546→    Hashtable with layout configuration (Mode, MaxPillarsPerScreen, GapSize, PreferredScreen)\n   547→\n   548→.EXAMPLE\n   549→    Set-MatrixLayoutConfig -Config @{ Mode = 'Quads'; GapSize = 80 }\n   550→\n   551→.NOTES\n   552→    Implements US-001 pattern: atomic write with Move-Item -Force\n   553→    Implements US-002 pattern: try-catch on JSON operations\n   554→    Preserves existing state data (lastSlots, windows array, etc.)\n   555→#>\n   556→function Set-MatrixLayoutConfig {\n   557→    param(\n   558→        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n   559→        [hashtable]$Config\n   560→    )\n   561→\n   562→    $matrixDir = \"$env:USERPROFILE\\Documents\\Matrix\"\n   563→    $stateFilePath = \"$matrixDir\\matrix_state.json\"\n   564→\n   565→    try {\n   566→        # Ensure directory exists\n   567→        if (-not (Test-Path $matrixDir)) {\n   568→            New-Item -Path $matrixDir -ItemType Directory -Force | Out-Null\n   569→        }\n   570→\n   571→        # Load existing state or create new (US-002 pattern)\n   572→        $state = @{}\n   573→        if (Test-Path $stateFilePath) {\n   574→            try {\n   575→                $stateJson = Get-Content -Path $stateFilePath -Raw -ErrorAction Stop\n   576→                $stateObj = $stateJson | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop\n   577→\n   578→                # 0x2;\n}\n\"@\n\n# Load System.Windows.Forms for screen detection\nAdd-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n\n# --- SCREEN TOPOLOGY DETECTION ---\n\n<#\n.SYNOPSIS\n    Detect all monitors and return their working areas (excludes taskbar).\n\n.DESCRIPTION\n    Returns an array of screen objects with index, position, size, and primary flag.\n    Working area excludes the taskbar and other docked windows.\n\n.OUTPUTS\n    Array of @{ Index, Left, Top, Width, Height, IsPrimary }\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    $screens = Get-ScreenTopology\n    # Returns: @(\n    #   @{ Index=0; Left=0; Top=0; Width=1920; Height=1040; IsPrimary=$true },\n    #   @{ Index=1; Left=1920; Top=0; Width=1920; Height=1080; IsPrimary=$false }\n    # )\n#>\nfunction Get-ScreenTopology {\n    try {\n        $allScreens = [System.Windows.Forms.Screen]::AllScreens\n        $topology = @()\n\n        for ($i = 0; $i -lt $allScreens.Count; $i++) {\n            $screen = $allScreens[$i]\n            $workingArea = $screen.WorkingArea\n\n            $topology += @{\n                Index = $i\n                Left = $workingArea.X\n                Top = $workingArea.Y\n                Width = $workingArea.Width\n                Height = $workingArea.Height\n                IsPrimary = $screen.Primary\n            }\n        }\n\n        return $topology\n    }\n    catch {\n        Write-Warning \"Failed to detect screen topology: $_\"\n        # Fallback to primary screen only\n        $primary = [System.Windows.Forms.Screen]::PrimaryScreen.WorkingArea\n        return @(\n            @{\n                Index = 0\n                Left = $primary.X\n                Top = $primary.Y\n                Width = $primary.Width\n                Height = $primary.Height\n                IsPrimary = $true\n            }\n        )\n    }\n}\n\n# --- WINDOW DISTRIBUTION ALGORITHM ---\n\n<#\n.SYNOPSIS\n    Distribute N windows across M screens with a maximum per screen.\n\n.DESCRIPTION\n    Evenly distributes windows across available screens, respecting MaxPerScreen limit.\n    Algorithm: floor division with remainder distributed to first screens.\n\n.PARAMETER WindowCount\n    Total number of windows to distribute\n\n.PARAMETER ScreenCount\n    Number of available screens\n\n.PARAMETER MaxPerScreen\n    Maximum windows allowed per screen (default: 4)\n\n.OUTPUTS\n    Array of integers representing window count per screen\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount 4 -ScreenCount 1 -MaxPerScreen 4\n    # Returns: @(4)\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount 5 -ScreenCount 1 -MaxPerScreen 4\n    # Returns: @(4) - overflow not handled in distribution, handled by layout strategy\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount 8 -ScreenCount 2 -MaxPerScreen 4\n    # Returns: @(4, 4)\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount 3 -ScreenCount 2 -MaxPerScreen 4\n    # Returns: @(2, 1)\n#>\nfunction Get-WindowDistribution {\n    param(\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [int]$ScreenCount,\n\n        [int]$MaxPerScreen = 4\n    )\n\n    if ($WindowCount -le 0) {\n        return @(0) * $ScreenCount\n    }\n\n    if ($ScreenCount -le 0) {\n        Write-Warning \"Invalid ScreenCount: $ScreenCount\"\n        return @()\n    }\n\n    # Initialize distribution array\n    $distribution = @(0) * $ScreenCount\n\n    # Special case: single screen - distribute ALL windows to it (allow overflow for multi-row)\n    if ($ScreenCount -eq 1) {\n        $distribution[0] = $WindowCount\n        return $distribution\n    }\n\n    # Multi-screen distribution: balance windows across screens\n    # Base windows per screen (floor division)\n    $windowsPerScreen = [Math]::Floor($WindowCount / $ScreenCount)\n\n    # Cap at MaxPerScreen for balanced distribution\n    $windowsPerScreen = [Math]::Min($windowsPerScreen, $MaxPerScreen)\n\n    # Remainder to distribute\n    $remainder = $WindowCount % $ScreenCount\n\n    # Distribute base count to all screens\n    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $ScreenCount; $i++) {\n        $distribution[$i] = $windowsPerScreen\n    }\n\n    # Distribute remainder to first screens (round-robin)\n    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $remainder; $i++) {\n        $screenIndex = $i % $ScreenCount\n        if ($distribution[$screenIndex] -lt $MaxPerScreen) {\n            $distribution[$screenIndex]++\n        }\n        else {\n            # If first screen is full, find next available screen\n            for ($j = 0; $j -lt $ScreenCount; $j++) {\n                if ($distribution[$j] -lt $MaxPerScreen) {\n                    $distribution[$j]++\n                    break\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return $distribution\n}\n\n# --- PILLARS LAYOUT STRATEGY ---\n\n<#\n.SYNOPSIS\n    Calculate Pillars layout: vertical columns arranged side-by-side.\n\n.DESCRIPTION\n    Arranges windows as vertical pillars (columns) with configurable gaps.\n    Supports multi-row overflow and multi-monitor distribution.\n\n    Layout logic:\n    - If windowsOnScreen <= MaxPillarsPerScreen: single row of N columns\n    - If windowsOnScreen > MaxPillarsPerScreen: grid with MaxPillarsPerScreen columns, multiple rows\n    - Gap spacing: (N+1) gaps for N columns, (M+1) gaps for M rows\n\n.PARAMETER WindowCount\n    Total number of windows to layout\n\n.PARAMETER Screens\n    Array of screen objects from Get-ScreenTopology\n\n.PARAMETER MaxPillarsPerScreen\n    Maximum pillars (columns) per screen in a single row (default: 4)\n\n.PARAMETER GapSize\n    Pixel gap between windows and screen edges (default: 60)\n\n.OUTPUTS\n    Array of @{ X, Y, Width, Height, ScreenIndex, WindowIndex }\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Get-PillarsLayout -WindowCount 4 -Screens $screens -MaxPillarsPerScreen 4 -GapSize 60\n    # Returns 4 rectangles in a single row, evenly distributed with 60px gaps\n#>\nfunction Get-PillarsLayout {\n    param(\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [array]$Screens,\n\n        [int]$MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4,\n\n        [int]$GapSize = 60\n    )\n\n    if ($WindowCount -le 0) {\n        return @()\n    }\n\n    if ($Screens.Count -eq 0) {\n        Write-Warning \"No screens available for layout\"\n        return @()\n    }\n\n    # Step 1: Distribute windows across screens\n    $distribution = Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount $WindowCount `\n                                          -ScreenCount $Screens.Count `\n                                          -MaxPerScreen $MaxPillarsPerScreen\n\n    $rectangles = @()\n    $windowIndex = 0\n\n    # Step 2: For each screen, layout its assigned windows\n    for ($screenIdx = 0; $screenIdx -lt $Screens.Count; $screenIdx++) {\n        $screen = $Screens[$screenIdx]\n        $windowsOnScreen = $distribution[$screenIdx]\n\n        if ($windowsOnScreen -eq 0) {\n            continue\n        }\n\n        # Step 3: Determine if single row or multi-row\n        if ($windowsOnScreen -le $MaxPillarsPerScreen) {\n            # Single row layout\n            $columns = $windowsOnScreen\n            $rows = 1\n        } else {\n            # Multi-row layout: 4 columns, multiple rows\n            $columns = $MaxPillarsPerScreen\n            $rows = [Math]::Ceiling($windowsOnScreen / $columns)\n        }\n\n        # Step 4: Calculate cell dimensions\n        # Formula: (N+1) gaps for N columns/rows\n        $totalHGaps = ($columns + 1) * $GapSize\n        $totalVGaps = ($rows + 1) * $GapSize\n        $cellWidth = [int](($screen.Width - $totalHGaps) / $columns)\n        $cellHeight = [int](($screen.Height - $totalVGaps) / $rows)\n\n        # Step 5: Place each window in grid\n        for ($i = 0; $i -lt $windowsOnScreen; $i++) {\n            # Calculate grid position (column-major order)\n            $col = $i % $columns\n            $row = [Math]::Floor($i / $columns)\n\n            # Calculate pixel position\n            # Formula: edge gap + (cell_index * (cell_size + gap))\n            $x = $screen.Left + $GapSize + ($col * ($cellWidth + $GapSize))\n            $y = $screen.Top + $GapSize + ($row * ($cellHeight + $GapSize))\n\n            $rectangles += @{\n                X = $x\n                Y = $y\n                Width = $cellWidth\n                Height = $cellHeight\n                ScreenIndex = $screenIdx\n                WindowIndex = $windowIndex++\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return $rectangles\n}\n\n# --- QUADS LAYOUT STRATEGY ---\n\n<#\n.SYNOPSIS\n    Calculate Quads layout: 2x2 grid with plus-shaped gap in center.\n\n.DESCRIPTION\n    Arranges windows in a 2x2 grid pattern with a distinctive plus-shaped (+) gap\n    in the center where horizontal and vertical gaps intersect.\n\n    Layout pattern (single screen, 4 windows):\n    ┌─────┐ GAP ┌─────┐\n    │  1  │     │  2  │\n    └─────┘     └─────┘\n       GAP   +   GAP\n    ┌─────┐     ┌─────┘\n    │  3  │     │  4  │\n    └─────┘     └─────┘\n\n    Window placement order: Top-Left, Top-Right, Bottom-Left, Bottom-Right\n\n    For fewer than 4 windows:\n    - 1 window: Top-Left only\n    - 2 windows: Top row (TL, TR)\n    - 3 windows: Top row + Bottom-Left\n\n    For more than 4 windows:\n    - Overflow to additional screens (if available)\n    - Each screen gets up to 4 windows in quad pattern\n\n.PARAMETER WindowCount\n    Total number of windows to layout\n\n.PARAMETER Screens\n    Array of screen objects from Get-ScreenTopology\n\n.PARAMETER GapSize\n    Pixel gap between windows and screen edges (default: 60)\n    This creates the plus-shaped gap in the center\n\n.OUTPUTS\n    Array of @{ X, Y, Width, Height, ScreenIndex, WindowIndex }\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Get-QuadsLayout -WindowCount 4 -Screens $screens -GapSize 60\n    # Returns 4 rectangles in 2x2 grid with 60px plus-gap\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Get-QuadsLayout -WindowCount 2 -Screens $screens -GapSize 60\n    # Returns 2 rectangles in top row only\n\n.NOTES\n    Plus-gap calculation:\n    - Each quadrant: (screen_dimension - 3*gap) / 2\n    - Three gaps: left edge, center, right edge (or top, center, bottom)\n    - Center gap creates the distinctive + pattern\n#>\nfunction Get-QuadsLayout {\n    param(\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [array]$Screens,\n\n        [int]$GapSize = 60\n    )\n\n    if ($WindowCount -le 0) {\n        return @()\n    }\n\n    if ($Screens.Count -eq 0) {\n        Write-Warning \"No screens available for layout\"\n        return @()\n    }\n\n    $rectangles = @()\n    $windowIndex = 0\n    $windowsPerQuad = 4\n\n    # Calculate how many screens needed (4 windows per quad per screen)\n    $windowsRemaining = $WindowCount\n\n    for ($screenIdx = 0; $screenIdx -lt $Screens.Count; $screenIdx++) {\n        if ($windowsRemaining -le 0) {\n            break\n        }\n\n        $screen = $Screens[$screenIdx]\n\n        # Determine how many windows on this screen (max 4 per quad)\n        $windowsOnThisScreen = [Math]::Min($windowsRemaining, $windowsPerQuad)\n\n        # Plus-gap calculation:\n        # Each dimension has 3 gaps: edge + center + edge\n        # Two quadrants share the remaining space equally\n        # Formula: (total - 3*gap) / 2\n        $halfWidth = [int](($screen.Width - (3 * $GapSize)) / 2)\n        $halfHeight = [int](($screen.Height - (3 * $GapSize)) / 2)\n\n        # Define quad positions: TL, TR, BL, BR\n        $quadPositions = @(\n            @{ X = $screen.Left + $GapSize; Y = $screen.Top + $GapSize; Label = \"TL\" },\n            @{ X = $screen.Left + (2 * $GapSize) + $halfWidth; Y = $screen.Top + $GapSize; Label = \"TR\" },\n            @{ X = $screen.Left + $GapSize; Y = $screen.Top + (2 * $GapSize) + $halfHeight; Label = \"BL\" },\n            @{ X = $screen.Left + (2 * $GapSize) + $halfWidth; Y = $screen.Top + (2 * $GapSize) + $halfHeight; Label = \"BR\" }\n        )\n\n        # Place windows in order: TL, TR, BL, BR\n        for ($posIdx = 0; $posIdx -lt $windowsOnThisScreen; $posIdx++) {\n            $rectangles += @{\n                X = $quadPositions[$posIdx].X\n                Y = $quadPositions[$posIdx].Y\n                Width = $halfWidth\n                Height = $halfHeight\n                ScreenIndex = $screenIdx\n                WindowIndex = $windowIndex++\n            }\n        }\n\n        $windowsRemaining -= $windowsOnThisScreen\n    }\n\n    # Force return as array (PowerShell unwraps single-element arrays)\n    return @(,$rectangles)\n}\n\n# --- CONFIGURATION MANAGEMENT ---\n\n<#\n.SYNOPSIS\n    Read layout configuration from matrix_state.json.\n\n.DESCRIPTION\n    Loads layout preferences from persistent state file. Returns defaults\n    if config is missing or invalid. Implements US-002 error handling pattern.\n\n.OUTPUTS\n    Hashtable with layout configuration:\n    @{ Mode = 'Pillars'; MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4; GapSize = 60; PreferredScreen = 0 }\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n    # Returns: @{ Mode = 'Pillars'; MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4; GapSize = 60; PreferredScreen = 0 }\n\n.NOTES\n    Default configuration:\n    - Mode: Pillars (vertical columns layout)\n    - MaxPillarsPerScreen: 4 (max columns per screen)\n    - GapSize: 60 (pixels between windows and edges)\n    - PreferredScreen: 0 (primary screen)\n#>\nfunction Get-MatrixLayoutConfig {\n    $stateFilePath = \"$env:USERPROFILE\\Documents\\Matrix\\matrix_state.json\"\n\n    # Default configuration\n    $defaultConfig = @{\n        Mode = 'Pillars'\n        MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4\n        GapSize = 60\n        PreferredScreen = 0\n    }\n\n    # Try to load existing state (US-002 pattern: JSON error handling)\n    try {\n        if (Test-Path $stateFilePath) {\n            $stateJson = Get-Content -Path $stateFilePath -Raw -ErrorAction Stop\n            $state = $stateJson | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop\n\n            # Extract layout config if present\n            if ($state.layout) {\n                $config = @{\n                    Mode = if ($state.layout.mode) { $state.layout.mode } else { $defaultConfig.Mode }\n                    MaxPillarsPerScreen = if ($state.layout.maxPillarsPerScreen) { $state.layout.maxPillarsPerScreen } else { $defaultConfig.MaxPillarsPerScreen }\n                    GapSize = if ($state.layout.gapSize) { $state.layout.gapSize } else { $defaultConfig.GapSize }\n                    PreferredScreen = if ($state.layout.preferredScreen -ne $null) { $state.layout.preferredScreen } else { $defaultConfig.PreferredScreen }\n                }\n                return $config\n            }\n        }\n\n        # No config found, return defaults\n        return $defaultConfig\n    }\n    catch {\n        Write-Warning \"Failed to load layout config from matrix_state.json: $_\"\n        Write-Warning \"Using default configuration\"\n        return $defaultConfig\n    }\n}\n\n<#\n.SYNOPSIS\n    Write layout configuration to matrix_state.json.\n\n.DESCRIPTION\n    Persists layout preferences to state file. Uses atomic write pattern (US-001)\n    to prevent corruption. Merges with existing state to preserve other data.\n\n.PARAMETER Config\n    Hashtable with layout configuration (Mode, MaxPillarsPerScreen, GapSize, PreferredScreen)\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Set-MatrixLayoutConfig -Config @{ Mode = 'Quads'; GapSize = 80 }\n\n.NOTES\n    Implements US-001 pattern: atomic write with Move-Item -Force\n    Implements US-002 pattern: try-catch on JSON operations\n    Preserves existing state data (lastSlots, windows array, etc.)\n#>\nfunction Set-MatrixLayoutConfig {\n    param(\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [hashtable]$Config\n    )\n\n    $matrixDir = \"$env:USERPROFILE\\Documents\\Matrix\"\n    $stateFilePath = \"$matrixDir\\matrix_state.json\"\n\n    try {\n        # Ensure directory exists\n        if (-not (Test-Path $matrixDir)) {\n            New-Item -Path $matrixDir -ItemType Directory -Force | Out-Null\n        }\n\n        # Load existing state or create new (US-002 pattern)\n        $state = @{}\n        if (Test-Path $stateFilePath) {\n            try {\n                $stateJson = Get-Content -Path $stateFilePath -Raw -ErrorAction Stop\n                $stateObj = $stateJson | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop\n\n                # Convert PSCustomObject to hashtable (preserve existing data)\n                $state = @{}\n                $stateObj.PSObject.Properties | ForEach-Object {\n                    $state[$_.Name] = $_.Value\n                }\n            }\n            catch {\n                Write-Warning \"Failed to load existing state, creating new: $_\"\n                $state = @{}\n            }\n        }\n\n        # Update layout section with provided config\n        $state.layout = @{\n            mode = if ($Config.Mode) { $Config.Mode } else { 'Pillars' }\n            maxPillarsPerScreen = if ($Config.MaxPillarsPerScreen) { $Config.MaxPillarsPerScreen } else { 4 }\n            gapSize = if ($Config.GapSize) { $Config.GapSize } else { 60 }\n            preferredScreen = if ($Config.PreferredScreen -ne $null) { $Config.PreferredScreen } else { 0 }\n        }\n\n        # Convert to JSON\n        $stateJson = $state | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10 -ErrorAction Stop\n\n        # Atomic write pattern (US-001): write to temp file, then move\n        $tempFile = [System.IO.Path]::GetTempFileName()\n        $stateJson | Out-File -FilePath $tempFile -Encoding UTF8 -ErrorAction Stop\n        Move-Item -Path $tempFile -Destination $stateFilePath -Force -ErrorAction Stop\n\n        Write-Verbose \"Layout configuration saved successfully\"\n    }\n    catch {\n        Write-Warning \"Failed to save layout config to matrix_state.json: $_\"\n        # Clean up temp file if it exists\n        if ($tempFile -and (Test-Path $tempFile)) {\n            Remove-Item -Path $tempFile -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n        }\n        throw\n    }\n}\n\n# --- MAIN ENTRY POINT ---\n\n<#\n.SYNOPSIS\n    Calculate and apply window layout to Matrix Terminal windows.\n\n.DESCRIPTION\n    Main entry point for the Window Layout Engine. Takes a collection of window handles\n    and arranges them using either Pillars or Quads layout strategy.\n\n    Pillars mode: Windows arranged as vertical columns side-by-side\n    Quads mode: Windows arranged in 2x2 grid with plus-shaped gap\n    Auto mode: Pillars for 1-4 windows, Quads for 5+ windows\n\n.PARAMETER WindowHandles\n    Hashtable where keys are shader names (e.g., \"Matrix-1\") and values are\n    hashtables with Handle and optionally ProcessId:\n    @{ \"Matrix-1\" = @{ Handle = 12345; ProcessId = 789 } }\n\n.PARAMETER Mode\n    Layout strategy to use: 'Pillars', 'Quads', or 'Auto' (default)\n    Auto selects Pillars for 1-4 windows, Quads for 5+ windows\n\n.PARAMETER DryRun\n    If specified, calculates and returns layout without moving windows\n\n.OUTPUTS\n    When DryRun: Array of @{ Handle, X, Y, Width, Height, ScreenIndex }\n    Otherwise: $null (windows are moved as side effect)\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowHandles $global:matrixWindowHandles -Mode 'Pillars'\n    # Arranges all tracked windows in pillars layout\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    $layout = Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowHandles $handles -DryRun\n    # Returns calculated layout without moving windows\n\n.NOTES\n    This function is the primary integration point for matrix_control.ps1,\n    matrix_setup.ps1, and bluepill.ps1.\n\n    Uses configuration from Get-MatrixLayoutConfig when Mode is not specified.\n    Handles edge cases: zero windows, invalid handles, minimized windows.\n#>\nfunction Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout {\n    param(\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [hashtable]$WindowHandles,\n\n        [ValidateSet('Pillars', 'Quads', 'Auto')]\n        [string]$Mode = 'Auto',\n\n        [switch]$DryRun\n    )\n\n    # Get valid window handles (filter out any invalid ones)\n    $validWindows = @()\n    foreach ($name in $WindowHandles.Keys) {\n        $entry = $WindowHandles[$name]\n        $handle = if ($entry -is [hashtable]) { $entry.Handle } else { $entry }\n\n        if ($handle -and $handle -ne [IntPtr]::Zero) {\n            # Check if window still exists and is visible\n            if ([WindowLayoutAPI]::IsWindowVisible($handle)) {\n                # Restore if minimized\n                if ([WindowLayoutAPI]::IsIconic($handle)) {\n                    [WindowLayoutAPI]::ShowWindow($handle, [WindowLayoutAPI]::SW_RESTORE) | Out-Null\n                    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 100\n                }\n\n                $validWindows += @{\n                    Name = $name\n                    Handle = $handle\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    $windowCount = $validWindows.Count\n\n    if ($windowCount -eq 0) {\n        Write-Verbose \"No valid windows to layout\"\n        return @()\n    }\n\n    # Get configuration\n    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n\n    # Determine effective mode\n    $effectiveMode = $Mode\n    if ($effectiveMode -eq 'Auto') {\n        # Use config mode, or default to Pillars for 1-4 windows, Quads for 5+\n        if ($config.Mode -and $config.Mode -ne 'Auto') {\n            $effectiveMode = $config.Mode\n        }\n        else {\n            $effectiveMode = if ($windowCount -le 4) { 'Pillars' } else { 'Quads' }\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Get screen topology\n    $screens = Get-ScreenTopology\n\n    if ($screens.Count -eq 0) {\n        Write-Warning \"No screens detected\"\n        return @()\n    }\n\n    # Get gap size from config\n    $gapSize = if ($config.GapSize) { $config.GapSize } else { 60 }\n\n    # Calculate layout based on mode\n    $layout = switch ($effectiveMode) {\n        'Pillars' {\n            $maxPillars = if ($config.MaxPillarsPerScreen) { $config.MaxPillarsPerScreen } else { 4 }\n            Get-PillarsLayout -WindowCount $windowCount -Screens $screens -MaxPillarsPerScreen $maxPillars -GapSize $gapSize\n        }\n        'Quads' {\n            Get-QuadsLayout -WindowCount $windowCount -Screens $screens -GapSize $gapSize\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (-not $layout -or $layout.Count -eq 0) {\n        Write-Warning \"Layout calculation returned no positions\"\n        return @()\n    }\n\n    # Sort windows by name to ensure consistent ordering (Matrix-1 before Matrix-2, etc.)\n    $sortedWindows = $validWindows | Sort-Object { $_.Name }\n\n    # Build result with handle-to-position mapping\n    $result = @()\n    for ($i = 0; $i -lt [Math]::Min($sortedWindows.Count, $layout.Count); $i++) {\n        $window = $sortedWindows[$i]\n        $position = $layout[$i]\n\n        $result += @{\n            Name = $window.Name\n            Handle = $window.Handle\n            X = $position.X\n            Y = $position.Y\n            Width = $position.Width\n            Height = $position.Height\n            ScreenIndex = $position.ScreenIndex\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Apply layout if not dry run\n    if (-not $DryRun) {\n        foreach ($item in $result) {\n            try {\n                [WindowLayoutAPI]::SetWindowPos(\n                    $item.Handle,\n                    [IntPtr]::Zero,\n                    $item.X,\n                    $item.Y,\n                    $item.Width,\n                    $item.Height,\n                    ([WindowLayoutAPI]::SWP_NOZORDER -bor [WindowLayoutAPI]::SWP_SHOWWINDOW)\n                ) | Out-Null\n\n                Write-Verbose \"Positioned $($item.Name) at ($($item.X), $($item.Y)) size $($item.Width)x$($item.Height)\"\n            }\n            catch {\n                Write-Warning \"Failed to position $($item.Name): $_\"\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return $result\n}\n\n<#\n.SYNOPSIS\n    Get the calculated layout for a given window count without window handles.\n\n.DESCRIPTION\n    Utility function to preview layout calculations. Useful for testing\n    and displaying layout information in the UI.\n\n.PARAMETER WindowCount\n    Number of windows to calculate layout for\n\n.PARAMETER Mode\n    Layout strategy: 'Pillars' or 'Quads'\n\n.PARAMETER Screens\n    Optional array of screen objects. If not provided, uses Get-ScreenTopology\n\n.OUTPUTS\n    Array of @{ X, Y, Width, Height, ScreenIndex, WindowIndex }\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Get-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowCount 4 -Mode 'Pillars'\n    # Returns 4 rectangle positions in pillars layout\n#>\nfunction Get-MatrixWindowLayout {\n    param(\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n\n        [ValidateSet('Pillars', 'Quads')]\n        [string]$Mode = 'Pillars',\n\n        [array]$Screens\n    )\n\n    if (-not $Screens -or $Screens.Count -eq 0) {\n        $Screens = Get-ScreenTopology\n    }\n\n    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n    $gapSize = if ($config.GapSize) { $config.GapSize } else { 60 }\n\n    switch ($Mode) {\n        'Pillars' {\n            $maxPillars = if ($config.MaxPillarsPerScreen) { $config.MaxPillarsPerScreen } else { 4 }\n            Get-PillarsLayout -WindowCount $WindowCount -Screens $Screens -MaxPillarsPerScreen $maxPillars -GapSize $gapSize\n        }\n        'Quads' {\n            Get-QuadsLayout -WindowCount $WindowCount -Screens $Screens -GapSize $gapSize\n        }\n    }\n}\n","numLines":843,"startLine":1,"totalLines":843}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"427b4202-7a06-41cf-b1e9-0be62d708b83"}

limit.\n    Algorithm: floor division with remainder distributed to first screens.\n\n.PARAMETER WindowCount\n    Total number of windows to distribute\n\n.PARAMETER ScreenCount\n    Number of available screens\n\n.PARAMETER MaxPerScreen\n    Maximum windows allowed per screen (default: 4)\n\n.OUTPUTS\n    Array of integers representing window count per screen\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount 4 -ScreenCount 1 -MaxPerScreen 4\n    # Returns: @(4)\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount 5 -ScreenCount 1 -MaxPerScreen 4\n    # Returns: @(4) - overflow not handled in distribution, handled by layout strategy\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount 8 -ScreenCount 2 -MaxPerScreen 4\n    # Returns: @(4, 4)\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount 3 -ScreenCount 2 -MaxPerScreen 4\n    # Returns: @(2, 1)\n#>\nfunction Get-WindowDistribution {\n    param(\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [int]$ScreenCount,\n\n        [int]$MaxPerScreen = 4\n    )\n\n    if ($WindowCount -le 0) {\n        return @(0) * $ScreenCount\n    }\n\n    if ($ScreenCount -le 0) {\n        Write-Warning \"Invalid ScreenCount: $ScreenCount\"\n        return @()\n    }\n\n    # Initialize distribution array\n    $distribution = @(0) * $ScreenCount\n\n    # Special case: single screen - distribute ALL windows to it (allow overflow for multi-row)\n    if ($ScreenCount -eq 1) {\n        $distribution[0] = $WindowCount\n        return $distribution\n    }\n\n    # Multi-screen distribution: balance windows across screens\n    # Base windows per screen (floor division)\n    $windowsPerScreen = [Math]::Floor($WindowCount / $ScreenCount)\n\n    # Cap at MaxPerScreen for balanced distribution\n    $windowsPerScreen = [Math]::Min($windowsPerScreen, $MaxPerScreen)\n\n    # Remainder to distribute\n    $remainder = $WindowCount % $ScreenCount\n\n    # Distribute base count to all screens\n    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $ScreenCount; $i++) {\n        $distribution[$i] = $windowsPerScreen\n    }\n\n    # Distribute remainder to first screens (round-robin)\n    for ($i = 0; $i -lt $remainder; $i++) {\n        $screenIndex = $i % $ScreenCount\n        if ($distribution[$screenIndex] -lt $MaxPerScreen) {\n            $distribution[$screenIndex]++\n        }\n        else {\n            # If first screen is full, find next available screen\n            for ($j = 0; $j -lt $ScreenCount; $j++) {\n                if ($distribution[$j] -lt $MaxPerScreen) {\n                    $distribution[$j]++\n                    break\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return $distribution\n}\n\n# --- PILLARS LAYOUT STRATEGY ---\n\n<#\n.SYNOPSIS\n    Calculate Pillars layout: vertical columns arranged side-by-side.\n\n.DESCRIPTION\n    Arranges windows as vertical pillars (columns) with configurable gaps.\n    Supports multi-row overflow and multi-monitor distribution.\n\n    Layout logic:\n    - If windowsOnScreen <= MaxPillarsPerScreen: single row of N columns\n    - If windowsOnScreen > MaxPillarsPerScreen: grid with MaxPillarsPerScreen columns, multiple rows\n    - Gap spacing: (N+1) gaps for N columns, (M+1) gaps for M rows\n\n.PARAMETER WindowCount\n    Total number of windows to layout\n\n.PARAMETER Screens\n    Array of screen objects from Get-ScreenTopology\n\n.PARAMETER MaxPillarsPerScreen\n    Maximum pillars (columns) per screen in a single row (default: 4)\n\n.PARAMETER GapSize\n    Pixel gap between windows and screen edges (default: 60)\n\n.OUTPUTS\n    Array of @{ X, Y, Width, Height, ScreenIndex, WindowIndex }\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Get-PillarsLayout -WindowCount 4 -Screens $screens -MaxPillarsPerScreen 4 -GapSize 60\n    # Returns 4 rectangles in a single row, evenly distributed with 60px gaps\n#>\nfunction Get-PillarsLayout {\n    param(\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [array]$Screens,\n\n        [int]$MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4,\n\n        [int]$GapSize = 60\n    )\n\n    if ($WindowCount -le 0) {\n        return @()\n    }\n\n    if ($Screens.Count -eq 0) {\n        Write-Warning \"No screens available for layout\"\n        return @()\n    }\n\n    # Step 1: Distribute windows across screens\n    $distribution = Get-WindowDistribution -WindowCount $WindowCount `\n                                          -ScreenCount $Screens.Count `\n                                          -MaxPerScreen $MaxPillarsPerScreen\n\n    $rectangles = @()\n    $windowIndex = 0\n\n    # Step 2: For each screen, layout its assigned windows\n    for ($screenIdx = 0; $screenIdx -lt $Screens.Count; $screenIdx++) {\n        $screen = $Screens[$screenIdx]\n        $windowsOnScreen = $distribution[$screenIdx]\n\n        if ($windowsOnScreen -eq 0) {\n            continue\n        }\n\n        # Step 3: Determine if single row or multi-row\n        if ($windowsOnScreen -le $MaxPillarsPerScreen) {\n            # Single row layout\n            $columns = $windowsOnScreen\n            $rows = 1\n        } else {\n            # Multi-row layout: 4 columns, multiple rows\n            $columns = $MaxPillarsPerScreen\n            $rows = [Math]::Ceiling($windowsOnScreen / $columns)\n        }\n\n        # Step 4: Calculate cell dimensions\n        # Formula: (N+1) gaps for N columns/rows\n        $totalHGaps = ($columns + 1) * $GapSize\n        $totalVGaps = ($rows + 1) * $GapSize\n        $cellWidth = [int](($screen.Width - $totalHGaps) / $columns)\n        $cellHeight = [int](($screen.Height - $totalVGaps) / $rows)\n\n        # Step 5: Place each window in grid\n        for ($i = 0; $i -lt $windowsOnScreen; $i++) {\n            # Calculate grid position (column-major order)\n            $col = $i % $columns\n            $row = [Math]::Floor($i / $columns)\n\n            # Calculate pixel position\n            # Formula: edge gap + (cell_index * (cell_size + gap))\n            $x = $screen.Left + $GapSize + ($col * ($cellWidth + $GapSize))\n            $y = $screen.Top + $GapSize + ($row * ($cellHeight + $GapSize))\n\n            $rectangles += @{\n                X = $x\n                Y = $y\n                Width = $cellWidth\n                Height = $cellHeight\n                ScreenIndex = $screenIdx\n                WindowIndex = $windowIndex++\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return $rectangles\n}\n\n# --- QUADS LAYOUT STRATEGY ---\n\n<#\n.SYNOPSIS\n    Calculate Quads layout: 2x2 grid with plus-shaped gap in center.\n\n.DESCRIPTION\n    Arranges windows in a 2x2 grid pattern with a distinctive plus-shaped (+) gap\n    in the center where horizontal and vertical gaps intersect.\n\n    Layout pattern (single screen, 4 windows):\n    ┌─────┐ GAP ┌─────┐\n    │  1  │     │  2  │\n    └─────┘     └─────┘\n       GAP   +   GAP\n    ┌─────┐     ┌─────┘\n    │  3  │     │  4  │\n    └─────┘     └─────┘\n\n    Window placement order: Top-Left, Top-Right, Bottom-Left, Bottom-Right\n\n    For fewer than 4 windows:\n    - 1 window: Top-Left only\n    - 2 windows: Top row (TL, TR)\n    - 3 windows: Top row + Bottom-Left\n\n    For more than 4 windows:\n    - Overflow to additional screens (if available)\n    - Each screen gets up to 4 windows in quad pattern\n\n.PARAMETER WindowCount\n    Total number of windows to layout\n\n.PARAMETER Screens\n    Array of screen objects from Get-ScreenTopology\n\n.PARAMETER GapSize\n    Pixel gap between windows and screen edges (default: 60)\n    This creates the plus-shaped gap in the center\n\n.OUTPUTS\n    Array of @{ X, Y, Width, Height, ScreenIndex, WindowIndex }\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Get-QuadsLayout -WindowCount 4 -Screens $screens -GapSize 60\n    # Returns 4 rectangles in 2x2 grid with 60px plus-gap\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Get-QuadsLayout -WindowCount 2 -Screens $screens -GapSize 60\n    # Returns 2 rectangles in top row only\n\n.NOTES\n    Plus-gap calculation:\n    - Each quadrant: (screen_dimension - 3*gap) / 2\n    - Three gaps: left edge, center, right edge (or top, center, bottom)\n    - Center gap creates the distinctive + pattern\n#>\nfunction Get-QuadsLayout {\n    param(\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [array]$Screens,\n\n        [int]$GapSize = 60\n    )\n\n    if ($WindowCount -le 0) {\n        return @()\n    }\n\n    if ($Screens.Count -eq 0) {\n        Write-Warning \"No screens available for layout\"\n        return @()\n    }\n\n    $rectangles = @()\n    $windowIndex = 0\n    $windowsPerQuad = 4\n\n    # Calculate how many screens needed (4 windows per quad per screen)\n    $windowsRemaining = $WindowCount\n\n    for ($screenIdx = 0; $screenIdx -lt $Screens.Count; $screenIdx++) {\n        if ($windowsRemaining -le 0) {\n            break\n        }\n\n        $screen = $Screens[$screenIdx]\n\n        # Determine how many windows on this screen (max 4 per quad)\n        $windowsOnThisScreen = [Math]::Min($windowsRemaining, $windowsPerQuad)\n\n        # Plus-gap calculation:\n        # Each dimension has 3 gaps: edge + center + edge\n        # Two quadrants share the remaining space equally\n        # Formula: (total - 3*gap) / 2\n        $halfWidth = [int](($screen.Width - (3 * $GapSize)) / 2)\n        $halfHeight = [int](($screen.Height - (3 * $GapSize)) / 2)\n\n        # Define quad positions: TL, TR, BL, BR\n        $quadPositions = @(\n            @{ X = $screen.Left + $GapSize; Y = $screen.Top + $GapSize; Label = \"TL\" },\n            @{ X = $screen.Left + (2 * $GapSize) + $halfWidth; Y = $screen.Top + $GapSize; Label = \"TR\" },\n            @{ X = $screen.Left + $GapSize; Y = $screen.Top + (2 * $GapSize) + $halfHeight; Label = \"BL\" },\n            @{ X = $screen.Left + (2 * $GapSize) + $halfWidth; Y = $screen.Top + (2 * $GapSize) + $halfHeight; Label = \"BR\" }\n        )\n\n        # Place windows in order: TL, TR, BL, BR\n        for ($posIdx = 0; $posIdx -lt $windowsOnThisScreen; $posIdx++) {\n            $rectangles += @{\n                X = $quadPositions[$posIdx].X\n                Y = $quadPositions[$posIdx].Y\n                Width = $halfWidth\n                Height = $halfHeight\n                ScreenIndex = $screenIdx\n                WindowIndex = $windowIndex++\n            }\n        }\n\n        $windowsRemaining -= $windowsOnThisScreen\n    }\n\n    # Force return as array (PowerShell unwraps single-element arrays)\n    return @(,$rectangles)\n}\n\n# --- CONFIGURATION MANAGEMENT ---\n\n<#\n.SYNOPSIS\n    Read layout configuration from matrix_state.json.\n\n.DESCRIPTION\n    Loads layout preferences from persistent state file. Returns defaults\n    if config is missing or invalid. Implements US-002 error handling pattern.\n\n.OUTPUTS\n    Hashtable with layout configuration:\n    @{ Mode = 'Pillars'; MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4; GapSize = 60; PreferredScreen = 0 }\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n    # Returns: @{ Mode = 'Pillars'; MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4; GapSize = 60; PreferredScreen = 0 }\n\n.NOTES\n    Default configuration:\n    - Mode: Pillars (vertical columns layout)\n    - MaxPillarsPerScreen: 4 (max columns per screen)\n    - GapSize: 60 (pixels between windows and edges)\n    - PreferredScreen: 0 (primary screen)\n#>\nfunction Get-MatrixLayoutConfig {\n    $stateFilePath = \"$env:USERPROFILE\\Documents\\Matrix\\matrix_state.json\"\n\n    # Default configuration\n    $defaultConfig = @{\n        Mode = 'Pillars'\n        MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4\n        GapSize = 60\n        PreferredScreen = 0\n    }\n\n    # Try to load existing state (US-002 pattern: JSON error handling)\n    try {\n        if (Test-Path $stateFilePath) {\n            $stateJson = Get-Content -Path $stateFilePath -Raw -ErrorAction Stop\n            $state = $stateJson | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop\n\n            # Extract layout config if present\n            if ($state.layout) {\n                $config = @{\n                    Mode = if ($state.layout.mode) { $state.layout.mode } else { $defaultConfig.Mode }\n                    MaxPillarsPerScreen = if ($state.layout.maxPillarsPerScreen) { $state.layout.maxPillarsPerScreen } else { $defaultConfig.MaxPillarsPerScreen }\n                    GapSize = if ($state.layout.gapSize) { $state.layout.gapSize } else { $defaultConfig.GapSize }\n                    PreferredScreen = if ($state.layout.preferredScreen -ne $null) { $state.layout.preferredScreen } else { $defaultConfig.PreferredScreen }\n                }\n                return $config\n            }\n        }\n\n        # No config found, return defaults\n        return $defaultConfig\n    }\n    catch {\n        Write-Warning \"Failed to load layout config from matrix_state.json: $_\"\n        Write-Warning \"Using default configuration\"\n        return $defaultConfig\n    }\n}\n\n<#\n.SYNOPSIS\n    Write layout configuration to matrix_state.json.\n\n.DESCRIPTION\n    Persists layout preferences to state file. Uses atomic write pattern (US-001)\n    to prevent corruption. Merges with existing state to preserve other data.\n\n.PARAMETER Config\n    Hashtable with layout configuration (Mode, MaxPillarsPerScreen, GapSize, PreferredScreen)\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Set-MatrixLayoutConfig -Config @{ Mode = 'Quads'; GapSize = 80 }\n\n.NOTES\n    Implements US-001 pattern: atomic write with Move-Item -Force\n    Implements US-002 pattern: try-catch on JSON operations\n    Preserves existing state data (lastSlots, windows array, etc.)\n#>\nfunction Set-MatrixLayoutConfig {\n    param(\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [hashtable]$Config\n    )\n\n    $matrixDir = \"$env:USERPROFILE\\Documents\\Matrix\"\n    $stateFilePath = \"$matrixDir\\matrix_state.json\"\n\n    try {\n        # Ensure directory exists\n        if (-not (Test-Path $matrixDir)) {\n            New-Item -Path $matrixDir -ItemType Directory -Force | Out-Null\n        }\n\n        # Load existing state or create new (US-002 pattern)\n        $state = @{}\n        if (Test-Path $stateFilePath) {\n            try {\n                $stateJson = Get-Content -Path $stateFilePath -Raw -ErrorAction Stop\n                $stateObj = $stateJson | ConvertFrom-Json -ErrorAction Stop\n\n                # Convert PSCustomObject to hashtable (preserve existing data)\n                $state = @{}\n                $stateObj.PSObject.Properties | ForEach-Object {\n                    $state[$_.Name] = $_.Value\n                }\n            }\n            catch {\n                Write-Warning \"Failed to load existing state, creating new: $_\"\n                $state = @{}\n            }\n        }\n\n        # Update layout section with provided config\n        $state.layout = @{\n            mode = if ($Config.Mode) { $Config.Mode } else { 'Pillars' }\n            maxPillarsPerScreen = if ($Config.MaxPillarsPerScreen) { $Config.MaxPillarsPerScreen } else { 4 }\n            gapSize = if ($Config.GapSize) { $Config.GapSize } else { 60 }\n            preferredScreen = if ($Config.PreferredScreen -ne $null) { $Config.PreferredScreen } else { 0 }\n        }\n\n        # Convert to JSON\n        $stateJson = $state | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10 -ErrorAction Stop\n\n        # Atomic write pattern (US-001): write to temp file, then move\n        $tempFile = [System.IO.Path]::GetTempFileName()\n        $stateJson | Out-File -FilePath $tempFile -Encoding UTF8 -ErrorAction Stop\n        Move-Item -Path $tempFile -Destination $stateFilePath -Force -ErrorAction Stop\n\n        Write-Verbose \"Layout configuration saved successfully\"\n    }\n    catch {\n        Write-Warning \"Failed to save layout config to matrix_state.json: $_\"\n        # Clean up temp file if it exists\n        if ($tempFile -and (Test-Path $tempFile)) {\n            Remove-Item -Path $tempFile -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n        }\n        throw\n    }\n}\n\n# --- MAIN ENTRY POINT ---\n\n<#\n.SYNOPSIS\n    Calculate and apply window layout to Matrix Terminal windows.\n\n.DESCRIPTION\n    Main entry point for the Window Layout Engine. Takes a collection of window handles\n    and arranges them using either Pillars or Quads layout strategy.\n\n    Pillars mode: Windows arranged as vertical columns side-by-side\n    Quads mode: Windows arranged in 2x2 grid with plus-shaped gap\n    Auto mode: Pillars for 1-4 windows, Quads for 5+ windows\n\n.PARAMETER WindowHandles\n    Hashtable where keys are shader names (e.g., \"Matrix-1\") and values are\n    hashtables with Handle and optionally ProcessId:\n    @{ \"Matrix-1\" = @{ Handle = 12345; ProcessId = 789 } }\n\n.PARAMETER Mode\n    Layout strategy to use: 'Pillars', 'Quads', or 'Auto' (default)\n    Auto selects Pillars for 1-4 windows, Quads for 5+ windows\n\n.PARAMETER DryRun\n    If specified, calculates and returns layout without moving windows\n\n.OUTPUTS\n    When DryRun: Array of @{ Handle, X, Y, Width, Height, ScreenIndex }\n    Otherwise: $null (windows are moved as side effect)\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowHandles $global:matrixWindowHandles -Mode 'Pillars'\n    # Arranges all tracked windows in pillars layout\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    $layout = Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowHandles $handles -DryRun\n    # Returns calculated layout without moving windows\n\n.NOTES\n    This function is the primary integration point for matrix_control.ps1,\n    matrix_setup.ps1, and bluepill.ps1.\n\n    Uses configuration from Get-MatrixLayoutConfig when Mode is not specified.\n    Handles edge cases: zero windows, invalid handles, minimized windows.\n#>\nfunction Invoke-MatrixWindowLayout {\n    param(\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [hashtable]$WindowHandles,\n\n        [ValidateSet('Pillars', 'Quads', 'Auto')]\n        [string]$Mode = 'Auto',\n\n        [switch]$DryRun\n    )\n\n    # Get valid window handles (filter out any invalid ones)\n    $validWindows = @()\n    foreach ($name in $WindowHandles.Keys) {\n        $entry = $WindowHandles[$name]\n        $handle = if ($entry -is [hashtable]) { $entry.Handle } else { $entry }\n\n        if ($handle -and $handle -ne [IntPtr]::Zero) {\n            # Check if window still exists and is visible\n            if ([WindowLayoutAPI]::IsWindowVisible($handle)) {\n                # Restore if minimized\n                if ([WindowLayoutAPI]::IsIconic($handle)) {\n                    [WindowLayoutAPI]::ShowWindow($handle, [WindowLayoutAPI]::SW_RESTORE) | Out-Null\n                    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 100\n                }\n\n                $validWindows += @{\n                    Name = $name\n                    Handle = $handle\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    $windowCount = $validWindows.Count\n\n    if ($windowCount -eq 0) {\n        Write-Verbose \"No valid windows to layout\"\n        return @()\n    }\n\n    # Get configuration\n    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n\n    # Determine effective mode\n    $effectiveMode = $Mode\n    if ($effectiveMode -eq 'Auto') {\n        # Use config mode, or default to Pillars for 1-4 windows, Quads for 5+\n        if ($config.Mode -and $config.Mode -ne 'Auto') {\n            $effectiveMode = $config.Mode\n        }\n        else {\n            $effectiveMode = if ($windowCount -le 4) { 'Pillars' } else { 'Quads' }\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Get screen topology\n    $screens = Get-ScreenTopology\n\n    if ($screens.Count -eq 0) {\n        Write-Warning \"No screens detected\"\n        return @()\n    }\n\n    # Get gap size from config\n    $gapSize = if ($config.GapSize) { $config.GapSize } else { 60 }\n\n    # Calculate layout based on mode\n    $layout = switch ($effectiveMode) {\n        'Pillars' {\n            $maxPillars = if ($config.MaxPillarsPerScreen) { $config.MaxPillarsPerScreen } else { 4 }\n            Get-PillarsLayout -WindowCount $windowCount -Screens $screens -MaxPillarsPerScreen $maxPillars -GapSize $gapSize\n        }\n        'Quads' {\n            Get-QuadsLayout -WindowCount $windowCount -Screens $screens -GapSize $gapSize\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (-not $layout -or $layout.Count -eq 0) {\n        Write-Warning \"Layout calculation returned no positions\"\n        return @()\n    }\n\n    # Sort windows by name to ensure consistent ordering (Matrix-1 before Matrix-2, etc.)\n    $sortedWindows = $validWindows | Sort-Object { $_.Name }\n\n    # Build result with handle-to-position mapping\n    $result = @()\n    for ($i = 0; $i -lt [Math]::Min($sortedWindows.Count, $layout.Count); $i++) {\n        $window = $sortedWindows[$i]\n        $position = $layout[$i]\n\n        $result += @{\n            Name = $window.Name\n            Handle = $window.Handle\n            X = $position.X\n            Y = $position.Y\n            Width = $position.Width\n            Height = $position.Height\n            ScreenIndex = $position.ScreenIndex\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Apply layout if not dry run\n    if (-not $DryRun) {\n        foreach ($item in $result) {\n            try {\n                [WindowLayoutAPI]::SetWindowPos(\n                    $item.Handle,\n                    [IntPtr]::Zero,\n                    $item.X,\n                    $item.Y,\n                    $item.Width,\n                    $item.Height,\n                    ([WindowLayoutAPI]::SWP_NOZORDER -bor [WindowLayoutAPI]::SWP_SHOWWINDOW)\n                ) | Out-Null\n\n                Write-Verbose \"Positioned $($item.Name) at ($($item.X), $($item.Y)) size $($item.Width)x$($item.Height)\"\n            }\n            catch {\n                Write-Warning \"Failed to position $($item.Name): $_\"\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    return $result\n}\n\n<#\n.SYNOPSIS\n    Get the calculated layout for a given window count without window handles.\n\n.DESCRIPTION\n    Utility function to preview layout calculations. Useful for testing\n    and displaying layout information in the UI.\n\n.PARAMETER WindowCount\n    Number of windows to calculate layout for\n\n.PARAMETER Mode\n    Layout strategy: 'Pillars' or 'Quads'\n\n.PARAMETER Screens\n    Optional array of screen objects. If not provided, uses Get-ScreenTopology\n\n.OUTPUTS\n    Array of @{ X, Y, Width, Height, ScreenIndex, WindowIndex }\n\n.EXAMPLE\n    Get-MatrixWindowLayout -WindowCount 4 -Mode 'Pillars'\n    # Returns 4 rectangle positions in pillars layout\n#>\nfunction Get-MatrixWindowLayout {\n    param(\n        [Parameter(Mandatory)]\n        [int]$WindowCount,\n\n        [ValidateSet('Pillars', 'Quads')]\n        [string]$Mode = 'Pillars',\n\n        [array]$Screens\n    )\n\n    if (-not $Screens -or $Screens.Count -eq 0) {\n        $Screens = Get-ScreenTopology\n    }\n\n    $config = Get-MatrixLayoutConfig\n    $gapSize = if ($config.GapSize) { $config.GapSize } else { 60 }\n\n    switch ($Mode) {\n        'Pillars' {\n            $maxPillars = if ($config.MaxPillarsPerScreen) { $config.MaxPillarsPerScreen } else { 4 }\n            Get-PillarsLayout -WindowCount $WindowCount -Screens $Screens -MaxPillarsPerScreen $maxPillars -GapSize $gapSize\n        }\n        'Quads' {\n            Get-QuadsLayout -WindowCount $WindowCount -Screens $Screens -GapSize $gapSize\n        }\n    }\n}\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":266,"oldLines":6,"newStart":266,"newLines":7,"lines":["         [int]$WindowCount,"," ","         [Parameter(Mandatory)]","+        [AllowEmptyCollection()]","         [array]$Screens,"," ","         [int]$MaxPillarsPerScreen = 4,"]},{"oldStart":277,"oldLines":8,"newStart":278,"newLines":8,"lines":["         return @()","     }"," ","-    if ($Screens.Count -eq 0) {","-        Write-Warning \"No screens available for layout\"","+    if (-not $Screens -or $Screens.Count -eq 0) {","+        Write-LayoutLog \"No screens available for Pillars layout\" -Level \"WARN\"","         return @()","     }"," "]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1d9b0b48-1496-4964-9322-45c8d4c6f4ee"}
